# Emoji Kitchen changes — 2026-11-02

**Summary:** Since the 2026-09-20 snapshot, Google's Emoji Kitchen gained **4,130** combos, dropped **1**, and redrew **0** existing ones. It now offers **151,129** combos across **621** emoji. Newly supported emoji: 🐘 elephant, 🫏 donkey. Sticker batches: 6, newest 20260728. Source: Google's pair list v13 (last modified Tue, 01 Sep 2026 19:30:00 GMT).

## New combos (4,130)

**By emoji:** 🫏 donkey (611), 🐘 elephant (609), ⏳ hourglass flowing sand (163), 👊 facepunch (161), 🖨️ printer (154), 🏎️ racing car (127), 🩷 pink heart (118), 🧱 bricks (83), 🍰 cake (79), ⚡ zap (77), 🫖 teapot (74), 🙌 raised hands (73), 🔎 mag right (71), 🧿 nazar amulet (71), 👍 +1 (69), 🚧 construction (69), 🍹 tropical drink (64), 🍉 watermelon (62), 🦀 crab (62), 💍 ring (61), 🛍️ shopping bags (55), 🐻 bear (50), 🐩 poodle (46), 🕊️ dove of peace (45), 🙂‍↔️ head shaking horizontally (44), 🌌 milky way (42), 🍕 pizza (42), 🔬 microscope (41), 🫠 melting face (41), 🦇 bat (38), 🦞 lobster (37), 🐭 mouse (36), 🐷 pig (36), 🦋 butterfly (33), 🏵️ rosette (32), 🥫 canned food (32), 🏒 ice hockey stick and puck (31), 🎨 art (28), 🪲 beetle (28), ♠️ spades (28), and 54 more emoji; 22 other combos.

---
Data: Google's own Emoji Kitchen pair list, the file behind the "emoji kitchen" widget in Google Search (https://www.google.com/logos/fnbx/emoji_kitchen/emoji_kitchen_pairs.13.pb); sticker images are located on gstatic.com. Combos are unordered emoji pairs; 'redrawn' means a newer dated folder now holds the sticker (found by a folder sweep).
